Digger Lid","id":28477638574250,"position":2,"preview_image":{"aspect_ratio":1.0,"height":3712,"width":3712,"src":"\/\/diggerlid.com\/cdn\/shop\/products\/Photo13-9-2022_80944am.jpg?v=1764620502"}},"requires_selling_plan":false,"selling_plan_allocations":[],"quantity_rule":{"min":1,"max":null,"increment":1}} Description Don't go topless with the DiggerShield. The pinnacle of weather protection for mini excavators. Tailor-made with precision-cut 6mm polycarbonate panels, the DiggerShield fits your machine like a glove, providing tinted protection against rain, wind, and sun (clear available by custom order). This premium, Australian-made solution requires no drilling, making it a durable and versatile investment. Enhance your brand visibility, maintain machine access, and ensure top-notch protection with DiggerShield. Invest in the best and weather any storm with confidence. Full kit breakdowns: Kubota U17-3 - 3 panels Kubota U25 - 3 panels Kubota U27 - 2 panels Kobelco SK17 & Yanmar Vio17 - 3 panels Kobelco SK20/25 & Yanmar Vio20/25 - 2 panels Bobcat E17z/ E20/ E20z - 2 panels Wacker Neuson EZ17 & CAT 301.7 dcr (before Next Gen) & John Deere E18zs - 3 panels Hitachi ZX17u - 2 panels Komatsu PC18 - Rear panel only Team up with our Quicky cover for down-time protection. your work ethic and productivity will soar! #keepworkingkeepearnin Specification - 6mm Australian made tinted polycarbonate (clear available upon request) - Packed with protection film, bubble wrap and in secure carton. - Stainless steel canopy clamps (externally clamped & DO NOT require drilling into the ROPS) - Stainless steel bolts, nuts & washers - Some kits come with laser cut & folded galvanised brackets for fixture to various machine or polycarbonate panels or magnets - Some kits include ferrite pot magnets for additional fixture (rated to 25kg ea.) - Production time allow approx. 1 week Shipping - Same day dispatch on orders before 12PM (AEST) (Australian Business days) - All orders are provided with a tracking number. Please check your email at checkout. AUSTRALIA: FREE shipping on orders over $399*. Orders under $399 and/ or containing *KAJO Grease Packs are calculated by weight at checkout. Express option available USA: FREE shipping on orders over $399 USD. Import duties and tariffs are included in your final price (DDP). REST OF WORLD: FREE shipping on orders over $399 AUD. Import duties, VAT and tariffs are charged by your country on arrival (DAP). Read our full Shipping Policy Returns - 30-day returns on full-price items, counted from the day you receive them - Change of mind: items must be unused, clean and in their original packaging. A 10% restocking fee applies and you pay the return shipping. - Wrong size? Unopened exchanges are replaced with new. - Faulty, damaged in transit or sent the wrong item? We'll make it right at no cost to you. Your rights under the Australian Consumer Law always apply. - To start a return, email info@diggerlid.com with your order number and the reason. Read our full Refund Policy Australian Made 🦘 The DiggerShield is designed & made locally (in Melbourne ). While we're still proud of the diversified sourcing of our complete product range, this one in particular is special to DiggerLid! indestructible 🧨 At 200 x breaking strength of glass, polycarbonate is super strong! If you manage to break a DiggerShield panel, you're doing something horribly wrong with your excavator! Coupled with stainless steel clamps & bolts, this kit isn't going anywhere Tailored fit 👌🏼 Every model is the result of rigorous R&D to ensure every detail of the machine has been accommodated for perfectly. The panels are CNC cut, drilled and folded to suit every curve, corner & panel including fuel fillers, engine bay doors and machine controls Cover UP Now! Pay later the most Frequently Asked Questions need more info? email info@diggerlid.com Is the DiggerShield safe for transport? Yes! The DiggerShield is suitable for float/ towing up to 100 km/h. We use 6mm polycarbonate & stainless steel clamps & bolts to ensure your panels are secure. We offer replacement hardware if any deterioration occurs. It should be the drivers responsibility to double check that all hardware & fastenings are secure prior to travel to ensure safety. Should i get clear or tinted polycarbonate? 99% of the DiggerShield legends opt for the tinted panels & we would only recommend clear panels for night works. The tint is a perfect mix of - not too dark on overcast/ gloomy days but a great level of protection from the heat on sunny days! I cant see my model offered, can you make it? The DiggerShield product requires substantial enquiry/ interest for a particular model to go ahead. This is due to the R&D required to master the design. If you can't see your model listed, please still feel free to get in touch so we can add your interest to our waitlist. (Please note - We will only produce current/ new models moving forward) How long will it take to produce? The DiggerShield kits are made to order. We generally allow 1 week for production & 1 week for courier shipping depending on location. Is it easy to install? The DiggerShield is a DIY install & yes, it is relatively easy to install. We'd recommend allowing 2 people approx. 1.5 hours for a first time install. Grab a mate & a couple of beers, watch our instructional video and off you go. Only basic tools required! Can I speak to someone? Absolutely, Our business contact number is 1300 661 881. Get in touch with us today! Engineered to protect you and your machine from the harshest elements, our PRO Enclosure helps keep your projects on schedule. With windows on the left, right, rear and lower front panels, you can get more work done in comfort, while keeping your machine looking clean and professional. The open front (top) provides ventilation and unrestricted visibility of your blade and working area. The PRO Enclosure is rugged and lightweight , making it ready for any job site. Built with 490 GSM PVC ripstop and trusted YKK hardware, the PRO Enclosure sets the standard for weather-beating excavator accessories. Choose from 6 universal, adjustable sizes to suit over 200 models of mini excavators. Use the SIZE GUIDE below to find the best fit for your machine. Specification Dimensions below. See size guide page for quick fit chart or correct instructions and selection for unlisted excavators Style 1 - Refers to machines where the the body (engine and counterweight) is flush and in-line with the ROPS canopy. In style 1 sizes, the length of the cover from top to bottom is longer at the rear/ right hand side. Style 2 - Refers to machines where the body (engine and counterweight) is larger than the ROPS canopy. In style 2 sizes, the length of the cover from top to bottom is shorter around the rear/ right hand side to allow for this. Size - refers to the dimensions of the ROPS canopy itself (not the weight of the machine. We gauge this by a wrap around measurement as explained in our video on the size guide page. Max dimensions around the canopy: Extra small = < 3760mm or 12' 4" Small = 3760 - 4180mm or 12'4" - 13'8 1/2" Medium = 4180 - 4480mm or 13'8 1/2" - 14'8 3/8" 490 GSM PVC coated polyester ripstop grey material Shipping - Same day dispatch on orders before 12PM (AEST) (Australian Business days) - All orders are provided with a tracking number. See Fitting Instructions Below Excavator MODEL NOT LISTED? Follow the guide to find the best fit for your machine Video How to measure Step 1 Style 1 or Style 2? Style 1 The canopy and body are inline. The cover material on style 1 can hang down without interference Style 2 Usually larger machines where the engine and/ or counterweight are bigger than the canopy (not in line). The cover material on style 2 is shorter to allow for this Step 2 Find the best size Carefully find the largest measurement required around the machine canopy accounting for any protruding components (mirrors or grab handles) and select the next size up. See examples tab for reference. X-Small A : 900mm or 35 1/2" B : 980mm or 38 1/2" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 3760mm or 148" Small A : 990mm or 39" B : 1100mm or 43 1/4" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 4180mm or 164 1/2" Medium A : 990mm or 39" B : 1250mm or 49 1/4" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 4480mm or 176 1/4" . Examples See the explanation Example 1 As shown, the Wacker Neuson EZ17 is a style 1 because the canopy and body are inline and the cover material can hang all the way down. When wrapping a tape around this machine, including the front grab handle and following the approximate shape of the roof, we find the measurement to be less than 3760mm (XS allows UP TO 3760mm). By following the instructions, we can confirm this machine suits a style 1 — extra small . Example 2 As shown, the Wacker Neuson EZ26 is a style 2 because it is a larger machine where the engine and counterweight is larger than the canopy/ not in line. When wrapping a tape around this machine including the front grab handle, we find the measurement to be more than 4180mm (S) but less than 4480mm (M). By following the instructions, we can confirm this machine suits a style 2 — medium . Your pricing seems too reasonable, is everything good quality? We know who uses our products and how they get used. We would only ever bring out high quality products for earthmovers. Our cover material is a heavy grade PVC, our DiggerShields are Australian made and our grease is made in Germany. Everything is trade tough and designed to improve your working days. All the info can be found on the grease cartridges page. Basically, the KAJO grease setup removes the spring plunger section and works purely by the suction of the gun. Now you can use screw top cartridges with no bleeding, airlocks, mess or waste. change tubes in the fraction of the time. Yes it's that simple and yes it works! Check it out NOW! Can I speak to someone? Absolutely, Our business contact number is 1300 661 881. Get in touch with us today! Designed to fit neatly over the front of your trailer, the Draw Bar Cover protects all key components in one hit. Once fitted, everything stays covered, clean, and out of the weather. It protects: Tow hitch Safety chains Trailer plug Breakaway battery Associated draw bar hardware Instead of having to clean mud, dust, or road grime every time you tow, your gear stays protected and ready to go. The Draw Bar Cover is made from the same UV-stable, heavy-duty PVC used across the DiggerLid excavator cover range. It’s designed to withstand sun, rain, mud, and ongoing weather without tearing or breaking down. Reinforced stitching and edges help the cover hold its shape and last long term. When you need access, the cover removes quickly and easily. There’s no complicated setup or fighting with straps, just pull it back, do what you need to do, and cover everything back up when you’re done. Your hitch stays protected, your plugs stay cleaner, and your gear lasts longer — especially in wet, dusty, or muddy conditions. Versatile Fit While ideal for plant trailers the Draw Bar Cover also works on: Caravans Boat trailers Horse floats Toy haulers General-purpose trailers Anywhere exposed draw bar components need protection, this cover earns its place. Stay padded. Stay comfortable. Working on the ground means mud, water and grease. The DiggerLid PRO Mat gives you a dry, padded surface wherever the job is. It has heavy-duty waterproof PVC outside, 10mm closed-cell foam inside and built in magnets so you'll never lose the 10mm socket again. It folds clean side in, dirt wipes straight off and packs into its own carry bag. OG – the padded, magnetic work mat in Signature Grey. PLUS – adds YKK side zippers so you can join mats into one big work surface. Comes in 4 colours, and the safety colours have reflective tape. Built for earthmovers, truckers, 4x4 enthusiasts, mechanics, campers and more. Anyone who works on the ground or in awkward and uncomfortable situations, this is for you. Your back and knees will thank you! TERMS OF TRADE The following terms of trade (“these Conditions”) apply to and form part of any sale between Total Outdoor Constructions Pty Ltd ACN 626 509 260 ABN 91 626 509 260 ("the Supplier") and the Customer for the sale of the Goods or Services by the Supplier to the Customer. Definitions and Interpretation 1.1 Where the context permits, the following expressions will have the meanings given to them: "Business Day" means any day other than a Saturday, Sunday or a designated Public Holiday in Victoria; "Customer" means a party acquiring Goods or Services from the Supplier; “Goods” means products supplied or to be supplied by the Supplier to the Customer from time to time. "Intellectual Property Rights" means all intellectual property rights, including all copyright, patents, trade marks, design rights, trade secrets, domain names, know how and other rights of a similar nature, whether registrable or not and whether registered or not, and any applications for registration or rights to make such an application; "PPSA" means the Personal Property Securities Act 2009 (Cth); and "Services" means services performed or to be performed by the Supplier for the Customer from time to time. 1.2 In the interpretation of these Conditions, unless specified to the contrary: (a) time is of the essence; (b) words importing the singular include the plural and vice versa; (c) words importing any gender include all other genders; (d) a reference to a natural person includes a company or other corporate body and vice versa; (e) a reference to any legislation, regulation, code or local law includes any modification, re-enactment or substitution of it; (f) the obligations on the part of a Customer who is a natural person includes his heirs, executors, administrators and assigns; and (g) the obligations on the part of a Customer which is a company or other corporate body includes its successors and assigns. 1.3 Any obligation imposed by these Conditions on a Customer comprised of two or more persons (whether natural, corporate or a combination of the two) binds them jointly and each of them severally. 1.4 The Customer may not, without the consent of the Supplier, raise a set off or counterclaim available to it against the Supplier in reduction of its liability under these Conditions. 1.5 Clause headings are for ease of reference only and are not intended to affect the construction or interpretation of these Conditions. 1.6 If the time for performing any obligation under these Conditions expires on a non-Business Day, then time is extended until the next Business Day. 1.7 A waiver by the Supplier of a default by the Customer under these Conditions will not constitute a waiver of another or continued default of the same nature or any other provision of these Conditions. 1.8 In the event that any or part of these Conditions is held to be unenforceable, the unenforceable part shall be severed and the remainder of these Conditions shall remain in full force and effect. 1.9 These Conditions embody the entire agreement and understanding between the parties concerning its subject matter, and succeeds and cancels all other agreements, understandings and representations concerning the subject matter of these Conditions. 1.10 In entering into these Conditions, the Customer has not relied on any warranty, representation or statement, whether oral or written, made by the Supplier or any of its employees or agents relating to or in connection with the subject matter of these Conditions. 1.11 These Conditions can be varied by either party at any time, provided written notice of the variation is given to the other party and upon receiving written notice of the variation, it is given the opportunity to negotiate the proposed variation. Such variation will apply to all Orders received after the date the proposed variation is agreed. 1.12 These Conditions are to be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Victoria and the parties submit to the exclusive jurisdiction of the state and federal courts located in Victoria. 2. Formation of Contract 2.1 The Customer must place an Order with the Supplier by selecting items on the website and pressing the “Buy It Now” button or by sending an Order to the Supplier by email, as the case may be and as directed by the Supplier from time to time. The Supplier reserves the right to accept or reject an Order for any reason including without limitation, unavailability of product, an error in the price, image or the product description or error in the Customer’s Order. Orders are deemed received by the Supplier at the time of successful transmission of the Order, and the Customer will thereafter be unable to cancel the order. A contract for the supply of Goods or Services by the Supplier to the Customer is formed when the Supplier accepts an Order. 2.2 These Conditions apply to and form part of every contract for the supply of Goods or Services by the Supplier to the Customer. Orders are only accepted upon and subject to these Conditions. 2.3 Any qualification, addition, variation or provision which conflicts with these Conditions which appears on the Customer’s Order shall not form part of the contract between the Supplier and the Customer unless such change is duly accepted by an authorised person of the Supplier or is otherwise confirmed by the Supplier in writing. 3. Pricing 3.1 Goods or Services are invoiced at the current prices published on the Supplier’s website or otherwise advised by the Supplier to the Customer. 3.2 All prices quoted are in Australian dollars. 3.3 All Goods or Services must be paid for by the Customer at the time of placing the Order except where the Supplier and Customer have agreed otherwise. 3.4 In order to pay for the Goods and Services, the Customer may be required to provide its credit card details or may be transferred to an external site operated by third parties such as banks, Google Pay, Apple Pay or PayPal. The Customer agrees that the third parties are responsible for processing and the security of the Customer’s payment, and the Customer hereby releases the Supplier from any liability in relation to such payment. and (b) each party retains the claims it has against the other in respect of prior breaches of these Conditions. 6. Title and Property in Goods 7.1 Title to and ownership of and property in the Goods shall pass to the Customer only upon the Supplier receiving payment in clear funds of the full purchase price and any freight charges, taxes and interest or other monies due and while the Goods remain the property of the Supplier the Customer holds them on trust for the Supplier and the Customer shall store or keep them in a manner which clearly identifies them as the property of the Supplier. 7.2 If the Customer defaults under these Conditions, all of the Customers rights, including the right to be paid any money, under any contract pursuant to which the Customer has hired, sold or otherwise provided the Goods or any part of them, whether separately or together with other things, to third parties are automatically assigned to the Supplier with effect from the date of the default, but only to the extent necessary to enable the Supplier to recover the amount owed to it by the Customer. 7.3 Until the Customer pays all amounts it owes to the Supplier: (a) the Customer must keep all Goods insured against theft, damage and destruction on behalf of the Supplier (and if the Customer fails to insure the Goods the Supplier may do so and invoice the Customer for the cost of insurance); ## FAQs – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/faqs

How do I know what will fit my machine? We have a new sizing guide available for mini excavators on our website This chart will lead you to the right cover for your machine. All products also have recommendations in the description and dimensions and specs in the product photos. still unsure? send us an email - info@diggerlid.com or give us a call - 1300 661 881 Can I speak to someone? Absolutely, Our business contact number is 1300 661 881. Get in touch with us today! Your pricing seems too reasonable, are they still good quality? We know who uses our products and how they get used. We would only ever bring out high quality products for earthmovers. Our material is a heavy grade and is sure to stand the test of time and our fixtures (Zippers, straps and buckles) are all heavy and thick to ensure a durable product that will improve your creature comfort for many years. Do you make custom covers? Our rapidly growing range now fits over 60 machines, so chances are we have a cover for you. We do not actually tailor make covers to order, but we still encourage you to get in touch with us with your machine details. You show us the interest and we will provide the solution. Do you make covers for other machines? Your best bet to see what’s available is to check our website and social media pages. If nothing you desire is there, the best thing to do is ASK. We save every enquiry and with more interest comes a bigger range. A solution for you may just be on the horizon! Can I tow with a DiggerLid on my machine? For our PVC products, according to our liability the short answer is no. We classify this “extreme use” and any faults that occur from this won’t be covered under warranty. This does not mean we don’t trust our own product as it simply comes down to user discretion (driving around the corner to the site @ 60km/h might be okay but Driving an hour on the freeway @100km/h certainly is not). Our NEW DiggerShield product is suitable for machine float which is available for a selection popular mini excavators. Do your excavator enclosures fog up? No they don't. We've designed the enclosures with a fully open front window to allow the best visibility and ventilation for the operator. Make sure you store DRY! If packed away wet the clear PVC can absorb some moisture temporarily but will dry and clear up with fresh air or in sun. Are your products UV stable Of course! Our products are designed to withstand any weather (Rain, snow, sun) Can I see your covers in person before I buy? Potentially! We have an expanding dealer network in Australia. Shoot us an email and we can try get you in touch with a local supplier. How can I trust your online store? Read our reviews and get in touch with our team (1300 661 881) or one of our customers! We have sold thousands of products to Australia and many other continents, supply to many trusted companies (Kennard's Hire, Supergroups, Clark equipment etc.) and plan on growing these avenues much more. There is just no way we can achieve this with dodgy products, shitty customer service or an unsecure online store. We are an authentic small Australian business working hard to help the earthmoving trade! We answer to every email and enquiry within 24 hours guaranteed. Not convinced? give us a call - 1300 661 881 How long will delivery take? All orders are fulfilled and shipped at 12pm daily (excluding weekends). All online orders are shipped via Australia Post (you can check their current regular or express service turn around here: https://auspost.com.au/service-updates/domestic-delivery-times Tracking is provided with every order so that you can check live delivery status. Do you Co-Brand? Potentially, but read below Some parameters to Co-Branding: minimum order quantity must be achieved Non negotiable full pre payment Great for dealership, hire or large fleet companies. 2-3 month turnaround

## About Us – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/about-us

G’day! Joel and Luke here – the founders and directors of Digger Lid. We’d love to tell you an epic story about how the Digger Lid came about… but in reality, it was just 2 blokes running a landscape construction business, who hated rocking up to site to find a wet seat on their digger every morning. We were innovative in the first months – the first digger lid in existence was a king size mattress plastic bag thrown over our Wacker Neuson EZ17 at night. Fast forward 2 years, many late nights, lots of R&D and you’ve got yourself the official Digger Lid (we promise it’s no longer a plastic mattress bag) – oh how far we’ve come! We have now sold thousands of Digger Lid’s to all over Australia and corners of the globe. We’re here so that you can #keepworkingkeepearning Cheers, Joel and Luke

- Heavy Duty PVC coated polyester ripstop material - Thick and heavy resin zippers (Mirco/ 1.7t Excavator Cover & Skid Steer Loader Covers) - Brass eyelet tie points for securing - Adjustable straps for making a snug fit and stop flapping. (Micro/ 1.7t Excavator Cover & Skid Steer Loader Covers) - Elastic draw-cord with cord-lock buckle (Quicky/ Universal Engine Covers) - Double Stitching PERFECT FOR HIRE COMPANIES! - To be able to provide a customer with a dry machine in the middle of winter is an absolute winner. ## Features of Enclosures – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/features-of-enclosures

- Tough 650gsm PVC coated Polyester ripstop material - Side and rear clear PVC windows. Roll up window coverings for operation and roll down for overnight & storage (This helps prevent interior UV penetration and condensation). - Fully opening front window for max vision/ ventilation while operating - Adjustable fitment via buckle straps to tension up the cover & suit a variety of makes and models of zero swing & conventional swing machines. - 12v access port allowing you to run your 12v accessories to the outside of your machine, like beacons. - Side opening vent to ensure the Digger Lid does not interfere with your machines cooling system - Fully double-sided zip for quick and easy side entry. - Brass eyelets to tie down securely during storage. - NOTE: This product is NOT suitable for machine float/ transportation. replacements/ refunds will NOT be granted for damage caused by this. see DiggerShield product for towable option.

## DiggerShield Features – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/diggershield-features

- Tailored fit (laser cut and folded to your machine models canopy profile) with 2 or 3 panels per specific machine. (Rear, right side and half panel on entry side where compatible) - A semi-permanent and longer lasting offering to our standard enclosures - Australian made , designed and owned (licenced under the AMC) - Super tough 6mm polycarbonate sheets with CLEAR or TINT option for the same price! - DIY installation (grab a mate) - No drilling or modifications required. The kit features various external brackets that clamp to the canopy and only then bolt to your screens predrilled holes - Suitable for machine float and transportation* - Maximum visibility - Great for ﻿company signage! ﻿ - Canopy still removable with this product installed for ultimate versatility and machine access - Designed to allow full access to fuel refilling, engine bay and other serviceable components of the machine - All required panels, hardware and accessories are included for installation (as a complete kit) - For this product, we ship via couriers including TNT & DHL. This is to keep your premium product safe & secure . When shipping Large and heavy cartons via air freight domestically & internationally, it does come at a cost. This, we provide to you in the quickest and safest way possible at cost price.

## Product Order Info – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/product-order-info

- Made to order on a weekly cycle. Orders taken to manufacturing up until Sunday 11:59pm to commence manufacturing on Monday. example: if your order is placed on a Monday - Sunday (day 1-7), it will commence production the following Monday (day 8) and then be shipped the following Monday (day 15). - Door to Door courier shipping. (based on State/Territory and Carton weight/ dimensions - 20-25kg 1200 x 1000 x 200mm). - Machine development based on current models on a popularity/ market basis - Installation required. Instructional installation video will be provided (2 people allow 1-2 hrs). - Tinted and clear options are priced the same. The material is tinted and not a film application - While polycarbonate has an impact strength of 250 times that of glass, it is sensitive to surface scratching . Make sure it is suitable to your work environment - Polycarbonate is a flexible material and can vibrate. DiggerLid provides over 20 (depending on machine) mounting points with universal, tailored or magnetic brackets. - Perfect to combo with our ‘Quicky’ cover. Not suitable for full machine covers or excavator operator enclosures due to material reaction and surface abrasion (PVC & polycarbonate)

## DiggerShield Disclaimer – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/diggershield-disclaimer

This screen kit is not a full cabin. The screen kit is intended for weather protection and comfort only. The screen kit is not a safety screen and does not meet any standard for forestry, Australian standard or impact resistance. The screen kit does not seal to or weatherproof all of the machine and parts of the machine and operator may still be exposed to the elements (parts that are already designed by the machine manufacturer to be exposed). The product is UV stable but is not intended to reduce the UV exposure to the user or machine. *Towing and transport should only be done with all provided brackets installed correctly and you must not exceed 100 km/h. It is also the customers responsibility to make sure the products are fastened, secured and safe for towing and transport. It is the customers responsibility to ensure their vehicle is suitable and able for transportation.

MEASUREMENT X-Small SMALL MEDIUM A : 900mm or 35 1/2" B : 980mm or 38 1/2" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 3760mm or 148" A : 990mm or 39" B : 1110mm or 43 3/4" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 4180mm or 164 1/2" A : 990mm or 39" B : 1250mm or 49" All 4 sides combined gives a total allowance around the canopy of up to 4480mm or 176 1/4" . BUY NOW Examples As shown above the Wacker Neuson EZ17 is a style 1 because the canopy and body are inline and the cover material can hang all the way down. When wrapping a tape around this machine, including the front grab handle and following the approximate shape of the roof, We find the measurement to be less than 3760mm. By following the instructions, we can confirm this machine suits a style 1 - Extra Small As shown above, the Wacker Neuson EZ26 is a style 2 because it is a larger machine where the engine and counterweight is larger than the canopy and is not in line. When wrapping a tape around this machine including the front grab handle, We find the measurement to be more than 4180mm but less than 4480mm. By following the instructions, we can confirm this machine suits a style 2 - Medium

## Accessibility Statement – DiggerLid
https://diggerlid.com/pages/accessibility-statement

Accessibility Statement At DiggerLid, we are committed to ensuring accessibility for all our customers, including those with disabilities. We strive to make our website and services inclusive, user-friendly, and compliant with applicable accessibility standards. This website utilizes various technologies that are meant to make it as accessible as possible at all times. We utilize an accessibility interface that allows persons with specific disabilities to adjust the website’s UI (user interface) and design it to their personal needs. Accessibility Standards Our website is designed to comply with the Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) 2.1 Level AA. These guidelines are recognized globally as the benchmark for web accessibility, focusing on creating content that is perceivable, operable, understandable, and robust: Perceivable - Information and user interface components must be presentable to users in ways they can perceive Operable - User interface components and navigation must be operable Understandable - Information and the operation of user interface must be understandable Robust - Content must be robust enough that it can be interpreted reliably by a wide variety of user agents, including assistive technologies We continue our efforts to constantly improve the accessibility of its site and services in the belief that it is our collective moral obligation to allow seamless, accessible and unhindered use also for those of us with disabilities. Feedback We welcome your feedback on how we can improve accessibility for all users. If you have suggestions or encounter barriers while using our website, please contact us. We are here to assist you. Email: info@diggerlid.com Phone: 1300 661 881 Live Chat: Available during business hours Thank you for shopping with DiggerLid. We are dedicated to providing an inclusive experience for all!

This gives him a padded, insulated surface for the ground-level work he already does. 58% of Australians injured at work in 2021-22 were men. 3 It keeps the mess out of the ute Dirty sides fold inward. Instead of rolling mud through every layer, the Pro Mat Plus folds like an accordion so the dirty undersides fold in on each other. Shake it off, fold it up and throw it in the ute. 4 Nothing disappears into the dirt Sockets stay put. Built-in rare-earth neodymium magnets hold sockets, bolts and small tools where he left them. Moulded YKK side zips join two or more mats when the job gets bigger. One mat for a service, two zipped together for the big jobs. 5 He'll use it well beyond the jobsite From the shed to the campsite. Built from tough, waterproof 490GSM rip-stop PVC, it handles machinery repairs, shed projects, roadside fixes, 4WD trips and weekends away. When it gets dirty, shake it off or hose it down. The reality Built for blokes who work with their hands You can't stop him taking on the hard jobs. You can give him a better surface to do them on. Give his knees, back and work gear a break this Father's Day. Customers also use their Pro Mats for 4WDing, camping and roadside repairs. Which colour should I choose? + Safety Orange is the high-visibility choice. Your pricing seems too reasonable, is everything good quality? We know who uses our products and how they get used. We would only ever bring out HIGH quality products for earthmovers. Our cover material is a heavy grade PVC tarpaulin, our DiggerShields are Australian made polycarbonate and our grease is made by KAJO in Germany. All info can be found on the grease product page. The KAJO grease setup removes the spring plunger section and works purely by the suction of the gun. Now you can use screw top cartridges with no bleeding, airlocks, mess or waste. Change tubes in a fraction of the time. Yes it's that simple and thousands of operators Australia wide have converted and are loving it! Check it out NOW! Can I speak to someone? Absolutely, Our business contact number is 1300 661 881. Your pricing seems too reasonable, is everything good quality? We know who uses our products and how they get used. We would only ever bring out HIGH quality products for earthmovers. Our cover material is a heavy grade PVC tarpaulin, our DiggerShields are Australian made polycarbonate and our grease is made by KAJO in Germany. Everything is trade tough and designed to improve your day as an earthmover! Do you make custom covers? Our rapidly growing range now fits over 200 machines, so chances are we have a cover for you. We do not custom make covers to order, but we still encourage you to get in touch with us with your machine details & we will provide the solution. How does your grease setup work? All info can be found on the grease product page. The KAJO grease setup removes the spring plunger section and works purely by the suction of the gun. Now you can use screw top cartridges with no bleeding, airlocks, mess or waste. Change tubes in a fraction of the time. Yes it's that simple and thousands of operators Australia wide have converted and are loving it! Check it out NOW! Can I speak to someone? Absolutely, Our business contact number is 1300 661 881.
